# Inspections target — Field-level contract

Companion to the [Target Solution Specification](solution-specification.md). Baseline is preserved separately. Proposed changes are not implemented. Exact existing metadata and partial dependencies remain traceable to [baseline evidence](../as-is/evidence.json). Null/omitted precision, timezone, population or physical binding is labelled Unresolved, not guessed. A modified row is the current target; the original remains only in baseline evidence.

## 1. Object inventory

Every entry has one numbered subsection below. Existing objects retain stable GUIDs. Proposed external interfaces have no assigned technical GUID and must be bound to the enterprise authority. No relationship target is an inheritance parent. Partial external fields do not purport to reconstruct the complete owning application.

## 2. Canonical field tables

Req is Y, N or Conditional. Conditional means the named stage/mode/rule in that row, enforced on the server; incomplete drafts can save where explicitly permitted. Exact display names occupy the Field column; internal names/IDs are in lifecycle cells. Scope references and automation IDs refer to the main specification.

Shared inherited conditions are specified on the declaring base with the concrete consumer explicitly named; fields are not redeclared on descendants. Published fields are read only, with temporary native generation helpers isolated as exceptions. Native framework/base filters and security remain effective unless an explicit target row supersedes them. Retained undocumented restrictions stay unresolved under P-10.

### 2.1 Action Plan Abstract

**Internal name:** `ActionPlans_ActionPlanAbsObject`. **Inheritance parent:** `FragApplication_AbCorrectivActiObject`.

**Directly declared fields:** `Activites`, `ActivitiesStart`, `ActivitProgress`, `AssessResults`, `CFWFindings`, `MActivitesOrder`, `MaxTasksOrder`, `NbActivites`, `NbOpenActivit`, `NbOpened`, `NbTasks`, `NCR`, `PermitAbstract`, `PersonResp`, `ProvenEffective`, `ReviewerComment`, `ReviewType`, `RiskAbs`, `StartDate`, `Tasks`, `TasksProgress`, `Template`, `VOCAbstract`.

**Classification / authority:** Extracted structure retained; proposed operating restrictions where stated; Existing shared framework or Inspections configuration. **Disposition:** Retain.

**Baseline identity:** edca9466-cdf8-4317-85c9-d3efb40373a2; E42; $.GraphData.Items[7].

Inherited declarations are defined once in the referenced inheritance-base subsection. Subtype-specific restrictions in shared rows apply to this concrete consumer; there is no duplicate storage.

### 2.28 Question

**Internal name:** `CLF_QuestObject`. **Inheritance parent:** `CLF_QuestAbstractObject`.

**Directly declared fields:** `AbsoluteOrder`, `Checklists`, `ChecklistType`, `DependsOn`, `ExistingSection`, `HasDependsOn`, `HelperChecklist`, `LastPublish`, `NbChecklists`, `PubQuestions`, `QuestionRef`, `References`, `TextReferences`, `UseExisting`, `UseReferences`, `VisDepNoAns`, `VisibleIfs`.

**Classification / authority:** Extracted structure retained; proposed operating restrictions where stated; Existing shared framework or Inspections configuration. **Disposition:** Retain.

**Baseline identity:** 92628acf-dcc3-41c3-b23f-198d83cba0ef; E338; $.GraphData.Items[124].

Inherited declarations are defined once in the referenced inheritance-base subsection. Subtype-specific restrictions in shared rows apply to this concrete consumer; there is no duplicate storage.

Every inherited business field in Question Abstract and Checklist Abstract is copied to the corresponding inherited value on the published instance unless specifically identified as runtime/system metadata. Runtime DateCreated is native publication time; DateModifiedHel is a system-derived helper rather than an author-editable value. New ContributesToOutcome, NegativeChoices, NegativeBoolean and ActionRequiredIfNegative fields follow the same author-to-published mapping. BranchConfirmed and ResponseOutcome belong only to operational answers and are not publication fields.

Temporary hCheckAnswer runtime helper writes in the native answer-generation mechanism are a deliberate compatibility exception to immutable question content. Serialize generation per issued inspection, clear helpers after use and prove isolation under P-03; do not use this exception to edit wording, response semantics or memberships.

## 4. Contract completeness and implementation limits

All documented fields have one canonical row; all inventory objects have a numbered section. That is documentation coverage, not proof that every target mechanism is executable. Missing baseline lengths/precision, read-only population, physical external bindings, native callback/transaction behavior and new expression syntax remain Unresolved under the main specification gates. The implementation contract cannot be marked fully validated until those gates close.
